### Fix 18 - What R code prints in the console margin is shown as result rows (findings 2, 23)
- Finding: the r tool's output went to the console through the evaluation's split sink, so `head(fuel)` rows, the
  `tapply()` values and `[1] "      33 fuel.csv"` sat at column 0 in full ink (`summary()` and `tapply()` only
  looked indented because R pads them); one tool block had rows at columns 0, 2 and 4, and the `[1] ...` row
  directly under `› y` read as R echoing the person's line. R prints at width 100, so wide output also wrapped hard
  to column 0 in an 80-column console (`05-steer-queued.png`, `12-question.png`, `pre-light/09-approval-ask.png`).
- Red (`test '^console-message-stream$'`, the new test with the margin mocked, on the previous commit's code): FAIL
  1 on the rows (`a`, `b\033` raw, the long `[1] "word ..."` whole at column 0); the other two failures of that
  first run were the test printing `eval_r()`'s visible value, fixed with `invisible()`.
- Green: `eval_open()` sets `st$echo` (tee and the margin) and `st$split` (tee without it) and keeps the console's
  width and its own caller's frame; the sink is split only where `st$split`. `eval_flush()` passes new output to
  `eval_echo()`, which (when its sink is on top) lifts it, writes each line at column 4, escaped, dim in the
  console's colours and cut to the width with the ellipsis, through `gptr_message_write(..., from = st$frame)`, the
  writer below this evaluation (a parent evaluation's, or the console), then restores the sink. The result keeps
  every byte. `test '^console-message-stream$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 73 ]`; two older tests that
  pinned `tool output` at column 0 in the margin now expect `    tool output`.
- Not taken: a 20-row cap (finding 2's suggestion); the console showed all output before and the person's own
  `!code` output should not be cut. Output now shows per top-level expression or before a notice, not per `cat()`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-82, 03 §6.17, D-245's visual review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER
  set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1027 findings`.
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 11's results decision and the File Structure row of `R/eval-core.R`.
- Lint: 4 files, no lints. Neighbours with the evaluator, r tool, CLI, sub-agent and gateway tests
  (`^(eval-|copy-eval|tool-r|console-|gptr-config|agent-run|agent-loop|agent-dispatch|session-object|subagent-team|
  gptr-gateway|cli-claude|cli-codex)`): FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 7066.

  cut never ran. Fix 17 also dropped the word still held, which hid received text: the redactor's tail after an

  abort-stream cases (the tail `world` and `[secret:PAUSE_TEST_TOKEN]` gone).

  (`Heavy cars, so par...`, `Fanning out HELDWORD...`). After a pause and an abort the tail reads

### Fix 23 - Rows are cut one at a time (found re-capturing the fixes)
- Finding (re-capture of c413c267, tmux 60x20, `final/tmux-60x20` transcript and cast): the code row
  `    plot(mpg ~ wt, data = fuel, pch = 19, col = "#6c8ebf",` is exactly 58 columns, the width, yet it was written
  without its `,` and with no ellipsis, because the next row was cut. cli 3.6.6's `ansi_strtrim()` of a vector
  re-tests every row in its trimming loop (`bad <- ansi_nchar(xt, "width") > width - tw`), so a row that fitted is
  shortened with the cut one but gets no ellipsis. Every vector cut in the console had the bug: the person's rows,
  code and diff rows, result rows, flagged rows and Fix 18's printed output.

- Implementation notes:
  - The row writes through `console_cat()`, as the spinner did, so a clear from inside an evaluation (a sub-agent's
    question, the pause menu's [b]) reaches the console. Inside `gptr_message_write()` it is given the plain writer
    instead: routed again through the evaluation's writer, `eval_read_sink()` sank the console again and the
    clear's spaces went into the capture (`        tool output` in test-console-notice.R).
  - Tests that drive a record mock `console_class()` (`local_live_console()` in `helper-console-input.R`), as an
    interactive R; it forgets the row when the test ends, so no clear reaches the test log. `written()` and
    `term_rows()` (a terminal's rows for `\r`, `\n` and `\033[K`) are there too. The tests that held the waiting
    row now hold the live row: test-console-{notice,process-launch,fences,render}.R and, found by the full suite,
    test-compaction-acceptance.R ("a delayed aborted checkpoint ...", which read the record's task).
  - D-240: in CRAN mode (`NOT_CRAN=false`, warm) the new blocks of test-console-input.R take 3 to 16 ms, so none
    needs `skip_on_cran()`; the changed blocks of test-console-render.R already skip on CRAN.
- Plan amendment (same commit): the clear is `\r\033[K` (the row resets its own band, and NO_COLOR writes no SGR);

## Task 19 - A question waits for typing to pause and keeps the draft (2026-10-22)
- Red (the new and changed tests on HEAD's code, 41452c3, in a `git archive` copy): `test '^console-input$'`:
  `[ FAIL 1 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 57 ]`, `could not find function "ask_ready"`; `test '^console-typing$'`:
  `[ FAIL 3 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 253 ]`: in case B the half second after `y` shows no `› use base R` (the row came
  back only at the next request); the new pty case never shows `question waiting` and never gets `REQ operator:
  ... yes END`. On HEAD the approval was drawn after `ye`: the `s` went to the composer, and the Enter meant to queue
  `yes` answered the approval with an empty line (`Allow r? Type y, a, p or n ...`), so `yes` stayed a draft, not
  kept (`gptr.history` off) and never sent.
- Green: `ask_ready(last_key, now)`, `console_ask_wait()` and `console_asked()` in `console-input.R`;
  `console_live_state()` gives `question` from the record; `console_before_message(kind = "prompt")` calls
  `console_ask_wait()` before it clears the row; `ui_console_permission()`, `ui_console_select()` and
  `ui_console_input()` call `console_asked()` on exit. `test '^console-input$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS
  63 ]`; `test '^console-typing$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 256 ]`.
  - `ask_ready()`: TRUE for no key, at 1 s and later; FALSE at 0, 0.5 and 0.99 s.
  - pty (TERM=xterm-256color, 80 columns): with the ask due about 0.3 s after `BEGIN`, `y`, `e` and `s` typed 0.3 s
    apart, then Enter: the row says `question waiting`, `Allow r?` is drawn at least 1 s after the Enter, its `› ` is
    empty, `y` approves and the next request ends `REQ operator: The user sent this message while you were working:
    yes END`; no `Not an answer`, no `Permission denied`. Case B (its call now `Sys.sleep(1); zz = 1`): within half a
    second of `y` the row shows `› use base R` again, while the call runs.
- Implementation notes:
  - The last key is the composer's `rd$compose$t`, the time it read the batch, so keys read late (after an
    in-process tool) can hold a question back up to a second. The wait draws the row once with `question waiting`
    (where it shows); later keys redraw it as `console_keys()` does.
  - The row comes back when the question returns, not after each read: drawn after a read, a question that asks
    again (an invalid answer, a paste) drew and cleared it between its notice and its chevron.
  - The first draft of the pty case typed `yes` a fixed 0.5 s before the ask. The fake provider waits its `gap`
    after each of the call's two deltas too, so the ask came 1.3 to 2 s after the key, and the wait never ran
    (traced in the child: `ask_ready(2.646, 3.903)`). The case now types key by key as the ask comes due.
- Plan amendment (same commit): `console_ask_wait()` does steps 1 and 2 (the last key `rd$compose$t`, the row's
  `question waiting` the record's `question`); `gptr_readline()` re-applies the modes; `console_asked()` on exit of
  the three console questions redraws the row; the pty case types `yes` key by key; Spec adds IC-82, 03 §6.17 and
  D-243; Files add `R/console-ui.R`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (Task 19's item; Tasks 20 and 21 pending), IC-77 (a question drawn once typing pauses;
  Task 26 pending) and IC-82 (`question waiting`; the row drawn when a T console's question returns), 03 §6.17,
  D-243's Task 19 note, the doc of `ui_console_read()`. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set):
  `All four plan checks passed`, `1052 findings (info 828, warn 224)` against Task 18's 1047:
  `contract_fn_undefined` for `ask_ready()`, `console_ask_wait()` and `console_asked()` in 04,
  `prose_mentions_undefined` for `console_ask_wait()` and `console_asked()` in P28, as for earlier tasks' names.
- Visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went to

## Task 21 - A draft left at the end of a run stays until sent (2026-10-22)
- Red (the new and changed tests on HEAD's code, 328a202, in a `git archive` copy): `test '^console-input$'`: `[ FAIL
  14 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 70 ]`: the reader called readline (`peter> ` among its prompts) and returned its answer
  (NA), drew no row, set no modes and left the draft. `test '^console-(typing|repl)$'`: `[ FAIL 14 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 |
  PASS 496 ]`: the Task 18 case kept `and label it` (`↳ kept in history ↑ · and label it`) and sent two requests;
  pty B, C and H never showed their drafts at the prompt and saw `kept in history` 3 times; pty I never showed
  `peter[auto]> use base`, and its Ctrl-C at the prompt gave `press Ctrl-C again to leave`; the macOS paste case
  never showed `peter> half`.
- Green: `console_idle(rd, prompt)` in `console-input.R`; the terminal reader's `read()` returns its `typed` lines,
  else, while the composer holds a draft, `console_idle()`'s line, echoing either after the prompt, else readline's;
  `console_queue_left()` leaves the draft in the composer after an idle or max_turns end. `test
  '^console-(typing|repl|input)$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 594 ]`.
  - Pure reader cases (mocked fd 0 and stty): `and label` with ` it`, Up, Ctrl-D, Tab and `x\177 the axes` then Enter
    returns `and label it the axes`; the row `\rpeter> and label it\033[K` is drawn 4 times (Up, Ctrl-D and Tab
    change nothing), readline is never called, stty logs `-g`, `-icanon -echo -iexten min 1 time 0`, `SAVED`, and the
    rows end `peter> and label it the axes`. At 30 columns the row is `peter> …bel both of the axes` and Enter writes
    the whole line. On the band (256 colours, dark): `\r\033[48;5;237mpeter> x\033[K\033[0m`. `ab`, DEL, Ctrl-U:
    readline at `peter> `, nothing kept, the row cleared. Ctrl-C on `and plot`: `↳ kept in history ↑ · and plot`, the
    history entry `and plot`, readline at `peter> `.
  - The Task 18 case: `and label it`, typed with the Tab follow-up `then plot`, waits through that run and Enter at
    the prompt sends it: requests `go`, `then plot`, `and label it`; `peter[auto]> and label it` once; no kept line.
  - pty I (TERM=xterm, 60 columns): `use base` typed while `ONE` streams shows as `› use base`; after the run
    `peter[auto]> use base`, `x` gives `use basex`, DEL and ` R` give `use base R`, Enter: `REQ use base R END`. `and
    plot` typed during `TWO` is at the prompt; Ctrl-C: `↳ kept in history ↑ · and plot`, then readline's
    `peter[auto]> `, where Up and Enter send it (`REQ and plot END`); `again to leave` never shows.
- Implementation notes:
  - The editor reads fd 0 with no time limit (`console_typed_chars(rd, -1L)`); processx's poll is interruptible
    (checked in a pty child: `poll(list(fd), -1L)` gave `INTERRUPTED` 1.2 s after Ctrl-C), so Ctrl-C reaches it. An
    empty read (an end of input) ends it like Ctrl-C.
  - Tab off a trigger word is dropped before the key machine, which would send the other kind.
  - The reader echoes the editor's line as it echoes a follow-up (one `console_write()`), so Task 25's band has one
    place to go; the editor only clears its row.
- Plan amendment (same commit): `console_idle(rd, prompt)` is the editor (its row the prompt and the draft's tail;
  Enter's line echoed by the reader as a follow-up is; Tab off a trigger word does nothing; Ctrl-C or an end of input
  lists the kept draft, and that Ctrl-C is not the first of the two that leave; `console_queue_left()` leaves the
  draft after an idle or max_turns end; until Task 25 only the editor's row has the band); pty I also has Ctrl-C and
  Up; pty B, C and H find their drafts at the prompt; the macOS paste case; the Task 18 case; Spec adds 03 §6.17 and
  D-243; Files add console-render.R and test-console-repl.R; the File Structure row of console-render.R adds 21.

## Review of Tasks 16-21: fixes (2026-10-22)
A review of Tasks 16-21 at 5ef34b4 filed 30 findings (the visual ones from screenshots of that build in every
console, tags `vr21` and `vb21`; the code ones from a simulated Windows run and loaded test runs; all in the session
scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept), some of them the same defect. Each fix below is its own commit; a finding a later
task owns amends that task's text; a finding not taken says why. Before fixing, the build was captured again (tag
`rf-pre`: tmux 60x20 and 80x24, headless T light, band light and dark, NO_COLOR and TERM=dumb, the `t20s` and
`t21i` variants) and each visual finding was looked at there or in the reviewers' captures of the same build.
The first neighbour run at 5ef34b4 failed once in test-console-typing.R ("a line queued while an answer streams
waits for its block to end", load average about 10), which passed alone; Fix 5 makes it independent of load.

### Fix 1 - R.app draws no live row (finding 1)
- Finding: since Task 17 R.app showed the whole agent transcript, answer, calls and approval, in its input colour
  (blue), the colour of the `source(...)` line; the post (d49e199) and final2 (e8a2ea8) builds were black. A write
  that starts with `\r` as the run's first output gives everything after it that colour there (the reviewers'
  probe: five plain `cat()` lines after such a row came out blue), and R.app's `\r` erases a line printed just
  before it in the same flush, so no redrawn row is safe there.
- Looked at: `vis/review-vr21/rapp-compare.png` (09-approved of post, final2, t17, aes21, rv1621 and vr21) and
  `vis/review-vr21/rapp-cr/probe2-big.png`.
- Red (`test '^console-wait$'`, the new test on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 2 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 85 ]`: under
  `platform_gui()` `AQUA` the request wrote `\r# thinking 0s - ctrl-c stops` padded, and `the$live_row` was set.
- Green: `console_live_start()` returns where `platform_gui()` is `AQUA`. `test '^console-(wait|spinner)'`:
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 143 ]`. R.app is captured in the visual check at the end.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-82, 03 §6.17, D-243's review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER
  set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1058 findings (info 828, warn 230)`, as at HEAD.
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 17's L row says R.app draws no row.
- Lint: 2 files, no lints. Neighbours (`^(console-|gptr-config|agent-run|agent-loop|agent-dispatch|session-object|
  arch-layers)`): FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4727.
- Deviations: the amendment above.

### Fix 2 - Tests that expect the UTF-8 glyphs say so, also on Windows (finding 24)
- Finding: on Windows `console_style()` gives ASCII outside Windows Terminal and RStudio, but blocks that expect the
  UTF-8 set (U+258C, U+00B7, U+203A, U+2026) only set `cli.unicode = TRUE`. test-console-input.R is on CRAN's list
  (D-240), so win-builder would fail it; the other blocks fail on the Windows NOT_CRAN lanes. Hosted CI never ran
  this branch (its workflows trigger on main and on pull requests).
- Red (the reviewers' simulation, `vis/review/win-sim.R`: `is_windows()` TRUE, `WT_SESSION` unset, CRAN mode and
  NOT_CRAN, on HEAD's tests): failing blocks in CRAN mode console-input 5 of 16, console-ui 1, console-interrupt 1,
  console-wait 1; with NOT_CRAN also console-ui 2 and console-render 1 (`# thinking 6s - 1 queued - esc stops`
  where `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued · esc stops` was expected, `>> ` where `› `).
- Green: `local_utf8_glyphs()` in helper-console-input.R sets `cli.unicode = TRUE` and mocks `is_windows()` FALSE,
  as the style tests of test-console-render.R already did; the six blocks of test-console-input.R that expect the
  glyphs, console-ui's frame and keys blocks, console-render's colour-depth loop, the menu block of
  test-console-interrupt.R and console-wait's row block call it. Simulation: 0 failing blocks in the five files in
  both modes. `test '^console-(input|ui|render|interrupt|wait)$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 1076 ]`.
- Not changed: the simulation's other failures are its own (a fake CLI named `Rscript.exe`, the macOS reader).
- Lint: 6 files, no lints. Neighbours: FAIL 2, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4725: pty I lost its Ctrl-C at the idle
  editor (finding 11, load average about 9; Fix 6), nothing else.
- Deviations: none.

### Fix 3 - The live row's clock in the console tests reads 0s on any runner (finding 26; tests only)
- Finding: Task 17's expectations pin the row's clock at `0s`, but the clock is wall time since the record was made
  (`rec$t0`), and the blocks run risk classification, highlighting and console questions before the row is drawn
  again; under 32 busy processes "a prompt clears the live row, keeps its task and a later tick redraws" read
  `thinking 1s` (1 of 3 runs).
- Red (scratch copy of test-console-wait.R with `Sys.sleep(1.1)` after the record is made, as on a slow runner):
  HEAD's tests `[ FAIL 9 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 78 ]` (`thinking 1s` where `0s`); this commit's
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 87 ]`.
- Green: `wait_record()`, `notice_record()` and the render block that reads the record set `rec$t0 = Inf`, so
  the row's seconds are `max(0, -Inf)`, 0, however slow the runner; every expected row stays as written. No code
  changes.
- Lint: 3 files, no lints. Neighbours: with Fixes 4 and 5 (one run, below). Deviations: none.

### Fix 4 - The ask-after-idle pty case does not depend on the child's speed (finding 25; tests only)
- Finding: in "a terminal: a question waits a second after the last key", the ask came due 1.3 to 2 s after
  `BEGIN` while `y`, `e`, `s` and Enter took 0.9 s, so a slowed child found the last key over a second old,
  never drew `question waiting`, and the wait timed out (1 of 4 runs beside 12 busy processes, 5 of 5 beside 32).
- Red (scratch copies of the case with the child slowed, its fake provider's `gap` raised): HEAD's case
  `[ FAIL 1 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 9 ]` (`pty$wait("question waiting")` FALSE); this commit's
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 11 ]`.
- Green: the answer streams for about a second before its call, and the letters of `yes` are typed one every
  0.3 s until the row says `question waiting` (at most 30), so the last key is always under a second old when the
  ask comes due; then Enter, and the relay is `REQ operator: ...` with the letters typed, read across a wrapped
  row. The `>= 1 s` check from Enter to `Allow r?` and the empty chevron stay.
- Lint: 1 file, no lints. Neighbours: with Fixes 3 and 5 (one run, below). Deviations: none.

### Fix 5 - The streaming queue-line case types its steer while the answer streams (found by the neighbour run)
- Finding: "a line queued while an answer streams waits for its block to end" typed its steer at the fourth look
  of the typing task; under load the answer had not started by then, so the queue line came before the paragraph
  (the first neighbour run at 5ef34b4: `""`, `>> use base R`, `""` after `word40`).
- Red (scratch copies of the case with the answer starting 0.5 s late): HEAD's case
  `[ FAIL 1 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 3 ]`, the same rows; this commit's `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 4 ]`.
- Green: the steer is typed once a record's last block is `text`, the answer's first row written, while the
  rest streams.
- Lint: 1 file, no lints. Neighbours (Fixes 3-5 together, from a `git archive` of the tree): FAIL 0, WARN 0,
  SKIP 1, PASS 4728. Deviations: none.

### Fix 6 - Ctrl-C is a key a T console's composer reads (finding 11)
- Finding: under load Ctrl-C in a T console was lost, in 6 of 19 headless runs at load average 40-57 (none of 4
  at 12-22): during a streamed answer the stream went on, no `? Paused` came, and the `c` meant for the menu was
  sent as a prompt (`and colour by gearc`); at Task 21's idle editor nothing happened and Ctrl-U then dropped the
  draft. The reviewers found isig on throughout and no SIGINT lost by processx, curl, `cat` or stty in 160 trials,
  so the race is in R or gptr. The first neighbour run of Fix 2 lost it too (pty I, at the idle editor, load
  average about 9). Visual review finding 18 had deferred Task 18's fallback to Task 28 until this failed.
- Red (`test '^console-(input|typing)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 18 | WARN 0 | SKIP
  0 | PASS 356 ]`: the stty logs lacked `-isig`, `console_keys(rd, s, "use base\003x")` signalled no interrupt
  (the byte was dropped), and the idle editor kept nothing at `" it\003x"`.
- Green: `tty_call(TRUE)` adds `-isig`; `console_keys()` applies the keys before a `\003` and then calls
  `rlang::interrupt()`, which signals an interrupt with R's `resume` restart, so the policy's menu opens and
  resumes as for SIGINT (the keys after it in the batch are dropped); `console_idle()` takes its Ctrl-C path, the
  draft kept. `eval_open()` turns isig on while in-process R code runs (`tty_isig_on()`, back off in
  `eval_restore()`; a question inside re-applies the call's modes, which say isig then), so Ctrl-C interrupts the
  code as before. `test '^console-(input|typing)$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 374 ]`, the pty cases G
  (Ctrl-C while `long` streams, the menu, `c`) and I (Ctrl-C at the idle editor) now through the byte;
  `test '^eval-'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 543 ]`; INFRA-03 (`CI=true`, real SIGINTs, piped):
  `test '^console-interrupt$'` `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 92 ]`.
- Not done: the soak (Task 28's S10, 2 x nproc busy processes) would slow the other sessions on this Mac; S10 now
  checks the fallback.
- Also: 04 §7.1 (`tty_call()`), D-243's review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set):
  `All four plan checks passed`, `1061 findings (info 828, warn 233)`, against 1058 the
  new names (`tty_isig_on()`, `tty_isig_off()`, `rlang::interrupt()`), as for earlier tasks' names.
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 18's terminal modes and Task 28's S10.
- Lint: 5 files, no lints. Neighbours: FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4735. Deviations: the amendment above.

### Fix 7 - A question's read that Ctrl-C or an end of input ends ends the terminal's row (finding 13)
- Finding: Ctrl-C (or an end of input) at a console question left the person's row open, as readline leaves it.
  In T, `console_asked()` then redrew the live row with a bare `\r` on that row and the refusal cleared it, so
  `› and colour` vanished from the screen and the transcript (`vis/review-vb21/askctrlc/t2/raw.bin`: `› and
  colour\r`, the row, `\r\033[K`, `    error: Permission denied: ...`); under TERM=dumb the refusal was glued to it,
  `› and colour by gear    error: Permission denied: ...`.
- Red (`test '^console-(ui|interrupt)$'`, the new tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 3 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS
  259 ]`: an NA read wrote nothing in T and D.
- Green: `ui_console_read()` and the pause menu's `console_ask()` write a newline before returning NA in a T or D
  console; an IDE console ends the input line itself, so L writes none. `test '^console-(ui|interrupt)$'`:
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 262 ]`. The probe is re-run in the visual check at the end.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (Task 19's item), D-243's review note.
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 19's step 4.
- Lint: 4 files, no lints. Neighbours: FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4741. Deviations: the amendment above.

### Fix 8 - A key typed before the pause menu's chevron showed answers it (finding 14)
- Finding: under load (NO_COLOR and TERM=dumb runs of the `t20s` variant) the menu's hint was printed at 59.448 s,
  the person answered `s` at 60.25 s, and readline drew `› s` at 60.528 s, after `gptr_readline()`'s stty: the line
  came back under 0.1 s, so IC-75's stale rule took it as typed before the question and sent the letter `s` to the
  agent as a steer (`↳ steer · not an answer`); `use the median` was then read as the menu's answer and rejected,
  as was `/exit`, and the run never resumed.
- Red (`test '^console-(interrupt|typing)$'`, the new tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 3 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS
  363 ]`: a stale `use the median` at the menu went to `console_typed_ahead()` (an error without a REPL), and an
  approval switched no stty before its rows.
- Green: `readline_answer(prompt, keep, accept)` takes a stale line that is one of `accept` (trimmed, lower case;
  TRUE for any) as the answer; the menu reads its key with `console_ask(c("", k, keys))`, keeping other stale text
  in the history (`console_keep()`, so it is listed, never sent), and the [s]/[f] text with `console_ask(TRUE)`.
  `tty_question(TRUE)` switches to the saved modes before a question's rows (`console_before_message(kind =
  "prompt")`, the menu) and `console_asked()` switches back, so readline starts as the question shows;
  `gptr_readline()` switches only outside such a question. `test '^console-(interrupt|typing|ui)$'`: `[ FAIL 0 |
  WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 557 ]`, the D-class drain test's stty log now `-g -echo SAVED -g <drain> SAVED -echo SAVED`
  (the saved modes before the rows).
- Also: 04 §7.1 (`readline_answer()`, `tty_question()`) and §15.10 IC-76 (Task 20's item), D-243's review note.
  `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1063 findings (info
  828, warn 235)`, the new names as before (Fix 7's plan amendment included).
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 20's menu read.
- Lint: 6 files, no lints (two doc lines over 100 columns rewrapped after the run). Neighbours: FAIL 0, WARN 0,
  SKIP 1, PASS 4748. Deviations: the amendment above.

- Finding: `/exit` typed at the ask tool's `Choose a number, or type your own answer` was sent to the model as the

### Fix 16 - The live row drops whole terms before it cuts text (findings 3, 19)
- Finding: Task 17's collapse cut the status from the right by characters. At 80 columns the newest queued
  message was whole but the row ended `↳ follow-up · then save the plot as plot.png…` (only ` · ↑ edit` was
  dropped, yet the person's text looked cut, `vis/review-vr21/pro-16-follow-up-queued.png`); at 60 the keys were
  cut mid-term, `ctrl-c p…` and `ctrl-c…`, a key without its verb; queued messages were cut mid-word.
- Red (`test '^console-input$'`, the new expectations on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 6 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 87 ]`.
- Green: `console_live_row()` builds the status from terms and drops them whole from the right until the row
  fits: the hint's (`ctrl-c pause`, `tab follow-up`, `enter steer`, or the queue view's `↑ edit`), then
  `question waiting`, then `<n> queued`; only then is the queued message cut with `…`, keeping its head (dropped
  when not even one of its characters fits), down to the block and the time. At 80 columns `▌ thinking 28s · 1
  queued   ↳ follow-up · then save the plot as plot.png`; at 40 `▌ thinking 6s   enter steer` and `▌ thinking
  6s   ↳ steer · use log(mpg)…`; a draft drops `1 queued` rather than cut it (`▌ thinking 6s   › and plot ...`).
  Candidates (Task 22) stay one free text. `test '^console-(input|wait|spinner|notice)'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 |
  SKIP 0 | PASS 305 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-82, D-243's review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set): `All four
  plan checks passed`, `1066 findings (info 828, warn 238)` (Fixes 13-15's amendments included).
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 17's collapse rule.

  test, and Fix 20's case, which the copy also held).
- Green: `draw()` sets `cli.num_colors = console_colors()` first, as `console_keep()`, `console_queue_line()`, the
  pause menu and the console questions do. `test '^console-input$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 95 ]`.
- Also: D-243's review note.

### Fix 27 - A Tab or Up that ends a key batch acts as the key (finding 27)
- Finding: `console_compose()` took Tab and Up as keys only as a batch of their own. Keys typed during an
  in-process `r` tool wait in the terminal and are read as one batch when it returns (D-242), so `then plot`
  and Tab gave a draft with a literal tab and no follow-up, and Ctrl-U then Up lost the Up; so did a Tab typed
  within 50 ms of the key before it. Checked: `console_compose(list(), "then plot\t", ...)` kept `then plot\t`.
- Red (`test '^console-input$'`, the new rows on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 15 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 99 ]` (three
  blocks of this fix; Fix 26's twelve cursor-key expectations, which the copy also held).
- Green: a batch without a newline that ends in Tab or Up applies its text with the existing loop, then the key
  to the resulting draft: `then plot` Tab sends a follow-up, `use @mtc` Tab completes, Ctrl-U Up on `x` takes
  back; `a\tb` stays text, and still at most one action a batch. The idle editor drops such a Tab off a trigger
  word, as it drops a lone one. `test '^console-(input|typing)$'`: `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 406 ]`, then
  with the idle case `test '^console-input$'` `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 114 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (Task 16's item), D-243's review note. Plan amendment (same commit): Task 16's batch rule.
- Lint: 2 files, no lints. Neighbours: FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4795. Deviations: the amendment above.

  (`tty_restore()`, a `stty` child) before readline takes the terminal, and a key that arrives in between is
  echoed. The path (console_idle()'s exit, tty_restore(), gptr_readline()) is as at 5ef34b4; in the casts the
  first key came 31 to 43 ms after the editor cleared its row in every build, readline's prompt 11 to 99 ms after
  it in the earlier tags (`t21`, `vr21`, `vb21`, `rv1621`) and 22 to 216 ms after it here, and no earlier
  capture of the scenario shows it. Keeping the echo off from the editor's end until readline has the
  terminal would close it, but it changes the modes of every hand-over to readline; left to the maintainer.
- Commits: 014f844 to 8adc427, one per fix and the amendment, each pushed after its own checks; then this record.

  child lost its place; the composer's Tab left `› use @mtc` and `› /mo`, and the idle editor's `use @mt`; the
  layer table named a file R/ does not have.

  `completion_shift()`; `console_reader()` installs the completer around `gptr_readline()` where `rd$complete` (not
  at `+ `) and `repl_main()` sets it on a readline tty (also with a caller-owned completer); `console_objects()`

  - The table, through `console_complete()`: `summarise the da`, `please /mo`, `mail a@b.com`, `cost is $5`, `use

  - libedit (CRAN macOS R) calls the completer with readline's word; the C code then reads only `comps` and
    `fileName`, so `console_complete()`'s `.assignLinebuffer()` and `.guessTokenFromLine()` for a `!` line may
    reuse utils' completion environment. `paste0(..., recycle0 = TRUE)` keeps an empty candidate list empty.
  - `+ ` is left out: the line there continues `!` code, so R's own completion (or the caller's) applies.

## Task 25 - A shaded input band where the background is known (2026-10-22)
- Red (the new and changed tests on HEAD's code, d996568): `test '^console-(repl|render)$'`: `[ FAIL 17 | WARN 0 |
  SKIP 0 | PASS 899 ]`: `background_reply()` and `tty_query()` did not exist (3 errors); the reader read at a plain
  `peter> ` and `... `, wrote no reset after a read or at Ctrl-C, and echoed a held line (`plot it\nand label`) off
  the band; `console_style()` gave the band in a D, L and P console as in T; the pty child wrote no query, so the
  harness's answer reached readline as typed text (`rgb:` shown twice) and no prompt was on the band.

  the query on stdout, fd 0 read until DA1's reply or 250 ms, the modes restored on exit). `R/utils-conditions.R`:
  `background_reply()` (1 to 4 hex digits a component, BEL or ST, Codex's luminance weights 0.299, 0.587, 0.114);
  `console_style()`'s band needs a T console (`console_class()`), its `band_dim` only the colours and a known
  background. `R/console-repl.R`: `repl_background(rd)`, which `repl_main()` calls in a T console that is not
  piped: an answer sets `the$background`; keys read with the replies go through `console_compose()` to the reader

## Steering audit and review extras: fixes (2026-10-22)
An audit of steering at 0d5db96 (Task 22), prompted by the maintainer (steering text sometimes not shown, or shown
badly, while the agent works; always a small place to type a steer; a sent steer shows where it was inserted; steers
recorded properly as an R script), filed 20 findings (numbered from 0); its verifiers confirmed 18. This branch takes
14 of them as units steer-1 to steer-14 (finding 5 split), after Task 25 and before Task 26; the recording and replay
findings (0, 2, 3, 10 and finding 5's record half) are doc units on claude/fix-review-doc. Findings 4 and 16 were not
confirmed: 4 (a steer left queued at the end of a script) is Task 15's documented behaviour, pinned in
test-console-interrupt.R; 16 (without the band a delivered steer looks like an answer) is the documented delivery
format, and its block and text tell it apart. Input 1 comes from the open finding of "Review fixes: result" above
(keys typed as the idle editor hands over to readline are echoed before its prompt). The review extras (auth-1,
auth-2, console-1 to console-4, sessions-16; sessions-7 goes with Task 26) come from the cheat-sheet review. Every unit
was re-checked at f4c6f91a (Tasks 22-25 done) and is its own commit.

### Steer 1 - RStudio and R.app offer the pause menu; RStudio's row says typed lines run next (audit finding 12)
- Finding: in RStudio and R.app a steer typed during a run vanished from the screen within a second and later ran as
  its own prompt, one run per line, under a row that said only `▌ thinking 52s · esc stop`. The vanishing is
  RStudio's own (the verifier's plain-R probe hides an entered line at the next output the same way), so gptr cannot
  keep the line on screen; but the policy was abort-only there although resuming an interrupt works, so a run gave
  the person no place to steer.
- Red (`test '^console-(interrupt|input|repl|wait|spinner-gui|spinner-width)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's
  code): `[ FAIL 9 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 636 ]`: `console_menu_available()` FALSE under RStudio and R.app (the
  policy aborted the run: no steer queued, no second request), the RStudio rows ending `esc stop`, the banner `Esc
  stops an answer`.
- Green: `console_menu_available()` adds `rstudio` and `.Platform$GUI` AQUA (R.app; Windows' Rgui, also front end
  `rgui`, stays abort-only), so Esc opens the menu and the banner says `Esc pauses an answer` (Fix 9 follows it).
  `console_live_state()` sets `typed` where `front_end()` is `rstudio`, and a row with no composer adds `typed lines
  run next` after the key, the first term the collapse drops: `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued · esc pause · typed lines
  run next` at 80 columns, `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued · esc pause` at 40, `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued` at 30; Positron's
  and the T rows are as before. One typed line stays one prompt: joining them after a run cannot work, as R cannot
  ask RStudio whether another line waits without blocking. `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 647 ]`.
- Not changed: the rows the unit named in test-console-spinner-gui.R and test-console-spinner-width.R come from a
  state the test passes (`pause = FALSE`) or from Rgui, not from RStudio's state, so they stay.
- Also: 03 §6.2, 04 §7.14 (`with_interrupt_policy()`) and §15.10 IC-82, D-243's steering review note.
  `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1086 findings (info 829,
  warn 257)`, as at Task 25.
- Plan amendment (same commit): the Steering section's L-class bullet, Task 17's L row, Out of scope's type-ahead
  bullet, and Task 27's `?peter` and vignette list (Esc pauses in RStudio and R.app). The unit's plan kept R.app
  abort-only unless the live check resumed there; it did, so R.app is in.
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went to
  `vis/src-s1` (`git stash create` 00598d7, RStudio only) and `vis/src-s1b` (ed4019e, R.app too; its `R/` equals
  this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s1` and `vis/lib-s1b`. A variant, `vis/scenario-s1`: the steer-display
  audit's scenario with `keys-rstudio.json` (Esc while thinking, s and a steer, two approvals, a typed line then Esc
  during the final answer, three typed lines) and `keys-rstudio-esc2.json` (the same without the typed line).
  - RStudio 2026.09, new instances (`vis/rstudio/capture.py`, stopped by pid, the person's config unchanged), tags
    `s1-light` and `s1-esc2`: Esc while thinking showed `? Paused` and its keys at an empty `› `; `s`, `? Steer`,
    `use base graphics only`, `↳ steer`, the row `▌ thinking 4s · 1 queued · esc pause · typed lines run next`; after
    the first tool's result `› use base graphics only` before `Steps received` (the events `take steer`, then
    `operator`). Esc during the final answer paused it between whole rows and `c` continued. Three lines entered
    during that answer vanished at the next output, as RStudio does, and after the run ran as three prompts, `Noted.`
    each, as documented.
  - In `s1-light` and `s1-light-b` the person typed `a draft line` during the answer and pressed Esc twice; no menu
    came, and the `c` meant for it ran as a prompt after the run. RStudio's own completion list had opened on `line`,
    so one Esc closed it and the other cleared the line. A plain-R probe in a new instance (`vis/rstudio/runs/
    s1-probe2`: `f()` busy in a loop, its calling handler reading a line and resuming) does the same; `abc` then Esc,
    Esc paused at once, there and in gptr (`s1-probe`). So in RStudio a third Esc pauses: RStudio's keys, not gptr's.
  - R.app: a plain-R probe in a new instance (`vis/scenario-s1-rapp`, stopped) resumed after an interrupt whose
    handler read `x` (`RESULT: finished after the interrupt`); then the gptr scenario (`vis/scenario-s1-rappgptr`,
    `lib-s1b`, tag `s1-rapp-L`, the esc2 keys) ran the whole flow, the person's text black, no row: the menu, the
    steer delivered at the boundary, the pause and `c` in the final answer, the three typed lines as three prompts.
    Its text equals RStudio's `s1-esc2` but for token counts. An earlier try (`s1-rapp-L-try1`) typed the second
    approval's `y` before it showed (the driver's 4 s anchor cap; `--within 25` after).
  - Terminal.app, a new window (default 80x24, the `scenario-t25` variant, `lib-s1b`, tag `s1`; closed after):
    complete, no residue, 14 cursor moves, and its text equals `t25`'s byte for byte: the T console is unchanged.
  - Looked at: `vis/shots/rstudio/s1-light-try1/04-thinking-row.png`, `07-menu-queued.png` and `12-approved-2.png`
    (the try's second approval offered `y` and `n` only, so its `a` was refused; the keys answer `y` now);
    `vis/shots/rstudio/s1-light/05-paused.png`, `10-delivered.png` and `16-paused-2.png`;
    `vis/shots/rstudio/s1-esc2/17-typed-3.png` and `19-typed-ran.png`; `vis/shots/rstudio/s1-probe/b-after-esc2.png`;
    `vis/shots/rstudio/s1-probe2/d-esc1.png`; `vis/shots/others/s1-rapp-L/09-delivered.png` and `13-paused-2.png`;
    `vis/shots/terminals/s1/app-default-80x24/14-long-answer-streaming.png`.
- Lint: 6 files, no lints. Neighbours (`^(console-|gptr-config|agent-run|agent-loop|agent-dispatch|session-object|
  arch-layers)`): FAIL 0, WARN 0, SKIP 1, PASS 4988, against 4979 after Task 25.

### Steer 2 - Positron's row says Ctrl-C, then s, steers; its banner says typed text waits as R code (audit finding 13)
- Finding: in Positron text typed while peter works never steers: Positron's console holds it as R code,
  cleared at the next Ctrl-C or run after `peter()` returns (the verifier's run: `Error: ! unexpected 'while' in
  "s1 while"`), while the row said only `▌ thinking 2s · ctrl-c steer`, so the person took their text for sent.
- Red (`test '^console-(input|wait|repl)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 8 | WARN 0 |
  SKIP 0 | PASS 501 ]`: the rows ending `ctrl-c steer` (the new `positron` live state at 80 and 40 columns, the
  four cases of the new row test, test-console-wait.R's Positron row) and no banner line in Positron.
- Green: where `steer` is set the term reads `<key>, then s, to steer`: `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued · ctrl-c, then
  s, to steer` at 80 columns, `▌ thinking 6s · 1 queued` at 40 and 30, and with no queue `▌ thinking 6s` below 41
  columns (the term goes whole, as every term does); ASCII `# thinking 6s - ctrl-c, then s, to steer`.
  `repl_banner()` adds `Text typed while peter works waits as R code; Ctrl-C, then s, steers.` where
  `front_end()` is positron and the menu is offered; not in a terminal or RStudio, nor where nobody can answer. A
  one-shot `peter("...")` has no banner, so the row is the cue there. No parser or poller for Positron's input.
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 509 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-82, D-243's steering review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER

### Steer 3 - A deferred prompt opens its run before a steer queued after it (audit finding 1)
- Finding: `peter(.run = FALSE)` queues its prompt as a follow-up, and `run_initial_input()` took the queued steers
  before it, so `gptr_steer(s, "...")` (a steer by default) then `gptr_wait(s)` opened the run with the bare steer:
  the model answered it without its task (the audit's live GPT-5.6: `What value or calculation would you like me to
  round?`), the prompt came after as its own request, and the record skipped the turn's first user message (the
  steer) and labelled the call's own prompt `## Follow-up:`. A deferred prompt has no step boundary before it, so by
  D-242 the steer belongs after it. Routed here, not to the doc units: the order is the queue's (Task 4 edited
  `run_initial_input()`), and the record needs no change once the order is right.
- Red (`test '^(gptr-sdk|agent-run|doc-blocks)$'`, the new tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 5 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 |
  PASS 1099 ]`, all in test-gptr-sdk.R's new case: two requests, not one; the first request's user messages only
  `Use only the mpg column` (source `steer`); no `## Steer:` line and a `## Follow-up:` line in the block. The guard
  (a deferred call alone records no `## Follow-up` line) and the leftover case in test-agent-run.R passed already.
- Green: without input the older queue head opens the run, by the items' `t` (`session_enqueue()` sets it): the
  first follow-up when no steer is older, and the steers sent after it join that first request at the loop's start
  poll, as user messages with source `steer` (the run has made no request yet, so not as relays); the steers go
  first when one is older (a leftover steer) or the follow-up has no `t`. One condition before the old loop, which
  is unchanged. `doc_turn_body()` is unchanged: the auditor's doc change would make every plain `.run = FALSE` call
  record `## Follow-up: <its own prompt>`. `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 1104 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-55, D-242's steering review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set):
  `All four plan checks passed`, `1086 findings (info 829, warn 257)`, as at steer 2.
- Plan amendment (same commit): the Steering section (a paragraph after the boundary text: a run started without
  input opens with the older queue head; a leftover steer still opens it) and Task 4's decisions.
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went to
  `vis/src-s3` (`git stash create` d08f812; its `R/` equals this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s3`; `lib-s2`
  is the build before it.
  - The audit's recording experiment (`review-steer-recording/exp9`: `gptr_doc()`, `peter(.run = FALSE)`,
    `gptr_steer(s, "Use integers.")`, `gptr_wait(s)` under Rscript), rerun in `vis/review-s3/before` and `after`:
    before, four requests whose first holds only `Use integers.`, and the block `k = 42L`, `## Follow-up: Store
    k = 42.`, `k3 = k * 3L`; after, two requests, each `Store k = 42. || Use integers.`, and the block
    `## Steer: Use integers.`, `k = 42L`.
  - A variant, `vis/scenario-s3` (no keys typed: `analysis.R` holds `gptr_doc()`,
    `peter("Summarise mtcars", .run = FALSE)`, `gptr_steer()` and `gptr_wait()`; the console sources it, then prints
    the file). Terminal.app, new windows (default 80x24, tags `s3` and `s3-before`, closed after): before, the
    answer to the bare steer, `1 queued` on the row, `› Summarise mtcars` in mid-run, 3 requests and
    `## Follow-up: Summarise mtcars`; after, `› Use only the mpg column` above `▌ thinking 0s · ctrl-c pause`, one
    answer, 2 requests and `## Steer: Use only the mpg column` in the block. Complete, no residue, no cursor moves.
    RStudio 2026.09, a new instance (`vis/rstudio/capture.py`, tag `s3-light`, stopped by pid, the person's config
    unchanged): the same, under `▌ thinking 1s · esc pause · typed lines run next`. The headless degraded modes (tag

### Steer 4 - A pause the code continues from is not reported to the model as an interrupt (audit finding 7)
- Finding: `eval_on_interrupt()`, the evaluator's innermost calling handler, pushed the `interrupt` event before
  the pause menu answered. After s, f, c or b the `r` call ran to its end and the person saw its normal result,
  while the model read `[interrupted by the user after 1.5 s; side effects may have occurred]` above `[1] 2`,
  then the steer relay, which invites it to rerun finished code; the session JSONL kept the line too.
- Red (`test '^(eval-core|eval-format|console-interrupt|tool-r)$'`, the new tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 4 |
  WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 559 ]`: in a run, an interrupt the outer handler resumed left `interrupted_after` set,
  an `interrupt` event and the interrupt line in the model text (test-eval-core.R), and the `r` call's result
  in the request after the menu's s matched `interrupted` (test-console-interrupt.R). The outside-a-run half
  (status `interrupt`, one event with the result's seconds) passed already.
- Green: `eval_on_interrupt()` flushes and notes `st$interrupt_seen`, the seconds of the latest interrupt, and
  pushes nothing. `eval_interrupted()`, which `eval_loop_catch()` calls where nobody else handles an interrupt
  (outside a run), sets `interrupted_after` from it (now when it hit between expressions), pushes the event and
  sets status `interrupt`. In a run an abort unwinds past the result and `tool_interrupted()` writes `Interrupted
  after N s; side effects may have occurred.` as before; a resumed evaluation has no event, status `ok` and
  `interrupted_after` NULL. No neutral `paused` note: nothing was lost. `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 563 ]`.
- Also: 04 §5.8 (`interrupted_after`: the seconds before the interrupt that ended the evaluation, NULL after one
  the menu resumed), D-242's steering review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set):
  `All four plan checks passed`, `1086 findings (info 829, warn 257)`, as at steer 3.
- Plan amendment (same commit): the Steering section (a bullet under typing during an in-process `r` tool: a
  pause-menu answer that resumes leaves the call's result as if it had not paused) and File Structure's
  `R/eval-core.R` row.
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went
  to `vis/src-s4` (`git stash create` 4de873b; its `R/` equals this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s4`;
  `lib-s3` is the build before it. A variant, `vis/scenario-s4`: the steering verifier's `r`-pause scenario
  (`keys-rpause.json`: Ctrl-C 1.5 s into `Sys.sleep(4); 1 + 1`, s, `use the median`; `keys-a.json`: the same
  with a, then a prompt `next`), which after `/exit` prints the last three messages of the request after the
  `r` call.
  - Headless pty runs (`vis/review-s4/runs`, 80x24): with s, after, `tool_result [1] 2` then `operator The user
    sent this message while you were working: use the median`; before, `tool_result [interrupted by the user
    after 1.5 s; side effects may have occurred] | [1] 2` then the same relay, and the session JSONL held the line
    once (none after). With a, before and after alike, `tool_result Interrupted after 2.7 s; side effects may have
    occurred.` then `user next`. The screen text, times masked, is identical before and after in both cases.
  - Terminal.app, new windows (default 80x24, tags `s4` and `s4-before`, closed after): complete, no residue, no
    cursor moves; `? Paused` under the `r` strip, `[1] 2` and `done · 4.2 s`, `› use the median` at the boundary,
    and the printed request: `tool_result [1] 2` after, the interrupt line before. Terminal's own text equals the

### Steer 5 - [b] keeps what the REPL holds in history; a prompt to the background run says it steers (audit finding 6)
- Finding: the pause menu's [b] ends no run in the foreground, so `console_queue_left()` never settled what the REPL
  held for after it. The reader sent each held follow-up as a new prompt, `route_continue()` turned it into a silent
  steer of the run that went on (a Tab follow-up joined the run, which by D-242 a follow-up never does), the T
  console's draft came back at the prompt, and every later prompt steered with no line. Medium, not high: only
  through the experimental [b], and nothing is lost.
- Red (`test '^console-(interrupt|repl)$'`, the new tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 7 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 386
  ]`: after [b] nothing kept in the history, no kept list after `running in the background`, the reader's `typed`
  and the composer left as they were (test-console-interrupt.R); no `↳ steer · <text>` under a prompt to a session
  that runs or waits (test-console-repl.R). The no-list case with nothing held, a refused send and an idle session
  passed already.
- Green: `console_background_settle()` (R/console-interrupt.R), called by [b] after its `running in the background`
  row and before the blank row: the reader's `typed` lines and a non-empty draft go to `console_keep()`, `↳ kept in
  history ↑ · summarise after · a draft`, silent when nothing is held; `typed` is emptied and the composer
  dropped. Steers already queued stay for the run. `repl_turn()` notes before the send whether the session runs or
  waits (`route_continue()`'s own test) and, once the send succeeded, writes `console_queue_line(s, text, "steer")`
  and returns, so at verbosity below 2 the session's earlier answer is not printed as if it answered.
  `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 393 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (Task 15's bullet), D-242's steering review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with
  R_LIBS_USER set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1088 findings (info 829, warn 259)`: the two new warnings name
  `console_background_settle()`, which P28's prose and 04 name and no plan's code defines, as for `console_keep()`
  and `console_queue_left()`.
- Plan amendment (same commit): Task 15's decisions (a bullet: [b] settles the held lines and the draft as an abort
  does; a later prompt still steers and says so; no `/wait`) and Task 27's `?peter` list (after background the
  queued text goes to the history, unsent, and another prompt steers the run).
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went to
  `vis/src-s5` (`git stash create` 2baf643; its `R/` equals this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s5`; `lib-s4`
  is the build before it. A variant, `vis/scenario-s5`: the steering verifier's bg scenario (`go bg`: an answer, an
  `r` call, a 6 s wait, a long answer), whose end prints the last request's last three messages and whether the held
  text reached any request; `keys.json` (after the `r` call `summarise after` and Tab, `a draft`, Ctrl-C, b, then
  `now compare the groups`, `!!gptr::gptr_wait(gptr::gptr_last())`, the run's end, `/exit`) and
  `keys-rstudio.json` (Esc, f and `summarise after`, Esc again, b, then the same).
  - Headless pty runs (`vis/review-s5/runs`, 80x24): after, `› b`, the menu's dim rows, `↳ kept in history ↑ ·
    summarise after · a draft`, a blank row, `peter[auto]> now compare the groups`, `↳ steer · now compare the
    groups`; the events `add steer now compare the groups`, then at `gptr_wait()` `take steer`; the third request
    ends with the relay of that steer alone, `held text in any request: FALSE`, and the record `## Steer: now
    compare the groups`. Before, `peter[auto]> summarise after` and `peter[auto]> a draftnow compare the groups`
    (the draft came back at the prompt and the next words joined it), both silent steers, both relayed to the
    model, and the record `## Steer: summarise after`, `## Steer: a draftnow compare the groups`.
  - Terminal.app, new windows (default 80x24, tags `s5`, `s5-b` and `s5-before`, closed after): complete, no
    residue, no cursor moves, the band on the prompt rows; the same screens. Terminal's text equals tmux 80x24's but

### Steer 6 - A question waits while a draft has text, and says where the draft went (audit finding 8)
- Finding: in a T console a question came due a second after the last key even in mid-sentence: it cleared the
  live row, the only place the draft showed, and the rest of the sentence became its answer. In the verifier's
  runs an ask got `(typed) loess fit` and an approval read ` no more than 5 bins` as a denial (`Permission
  denied: more than 5 bins`), and the draft then took the next keys at the prompt (`please use the2`). A gap in
  D-243's Task 19 design.
- Red (`test '^console-(input|ui|typing)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 11 | WARN 0 |
  SKIP 0 | PASS 633 ]`: `ask_ready()` took no draft (an error); no `draft kept` row from `ui_console_read()` (3);
  in the 1 s pty case the question came within 1.5 s of the last key while `yes` was the draft, and the four
  expectations after it failed with it (5); no `draft kept` row at case B's approval and case H's question (2).
- Green: `ask_ready(last_key, now, draft = "")` waits 3 s while `draft` has text, else 1 s, and

### Steer 7 - A paste read on the polling beat stays one draft (audit finding 14)
- Finding: in a T console a multi-line paste without a final newline, typed while peter works, was split by
  the batch rule: its first lines went at once as a steer, its last line stayed the draft, so one message
  reached the model as two relays, the second after an Enter the person had to find. It contradicted the
  idle prompt's paste rule (`pasted: Enter sends it as one message`).
- Red (`test '^console-(input|typing|repl)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 17 | WARN 0 |
  SKIP 0 | PASS 716 ]`: test-console-input.R's nine `compose()` cases (`console_compose()` took no `paste`);
  the cadence case (4: the read 0.05 s after the last sent `b\nc` and kept `d`); the new pty case (4: no
  `› a b c` on the row, `queued` 6 times by then, no `REQ 1 [a/b/c]`, two requests after the first).
- Green: `console_compose(..., paste = FALSE)`: with `paste`, a batch with text after its last newline (not
  only edit keys) keeps its newlines in the draft, leading blank lines dropped; Enter later sends it as one
  message, a `!` one as a follow-up. `console_keys()` notes each read's time in `rd$polled`, empty reads too,
  and passes `paste` when the last read was at most 0.2 s before; the idle editor passes TRUE; the
  background probe keeps FALSE. A run's first read and the keys piled up during an in-process tool keep the
  old rule. `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 0 | PASS 748 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (the key machine), D-243's steering review note. `python3 -I dev/ci/check-plans.py`
  (with R_LIBS_USER set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1088 findings (info 829, warn 259)`, as at steer 6.

  Constraints' timing list.
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went
  to `vis/src-s7` (`git stash create` 85add83; its `R/` equals this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s7`;
  `lib-s6` is the build before it. A variant, `vis/scenario-s7`: the paste verifier's scenario with its two key
  files in one session (`keys-clean.json`: `p1 first line`, `p1 second line`, `p1 third line` pasted in one
  write during the slow pause-test answer, Enter 0.8 s later; `keys-frozen.json`: `x one`, Enter, `half two`
  typed while the approved 4 s `r` call runs, then Ctrl-C, a, `/exit`), event-anchored.

    no cursor moves, no stale capture; the same screens, the steer block on the band. With times, token counts

### Steer 8 - Keys after Ctrl-C in the same read answer the pause menu; text typed at it is kept (audit finding 9)
- Finding: `console_keys()` cut a read batch at Ctrl-C and dropped what followed, so under load Ctrl-C, `s`
  and Enter in one read paused without the `s`; the steer the person then typed at `? Paused` got
  `Paused. Type s, f, c, a or b.` and was kept nowhere (the verifier's `ctrlcbatch-T`: no relay, no
  `input` event). Low: the menu still answers a key typed again.
- Red (`test '^console-(input|interrupt|typing)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 19 |
  WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 562 ]`: the menu case (4: no `Not a key` row, nothing kept, gptr.history on and off);
  the `console_ahead()` table (an error: no such function); the Ctrl-C unit case (3: no `after_interrupt`,
  nothing typed after the menu); the new abort case (3: no `› a`, no draft at the prompt, nothing kept); the
  new pty case (8: no `› s`, no `? Steer`, the steer lost, `Paused. Type` twice).
- Green: `console_keys()` keeps the keys after Ctrl-C in `rd$after_interrupt` (a further Ctrl-C dropped).
  `console_ask()` first asks `console_ahead(rd, accept)`: the first whole line, its edit keys applied
  (`console_compose()`), that is one of `accept` (TRUE for [s]'s and [f]'s text) answers, echoed `› s` at
  the chevron; the lines before it, or all the text when none answers (an unterminated key too), go to
  `console_keep()`; the rest waits for the next question. Once the menu resumes, `console_keys()` reads what
  it left as keys typed after it; after [a] `console_send()` makes it the draft at the next prompt. A non-key
  answer is kept in the history in an interactive R, as an approval keeps one: `    Not a key: 'add a
  legend' (kept in history).` above `    Paused. Type s, f, c, a or b.`; it is never sent. `[ FAIL 0 |
  WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 589 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-76 (Task 20's paragraph), D-243's steering review note. `python3 -I
  dev/ci/check-plans.py` (with R_LIBS_USER set): `All four plan checks passed`, `1090 findings (info 829,
  warn 261)`: the two new warnings name `console_ahead()`, which P28's prose and 04 name and no plan's code
  defines, as for `console_background_settle()`.

- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went

  - Headless pty runs (`vis/review-s8/runs`, 80x24): after, under the keys row `› s`, `? Steer`, `› use the

### Steer 9 - TERM=dumb: a held queue line gives way to its steer's delivery (audit finding 15)
- Finding: in a D console (TERM=dumb) a steer typed while the answer streamed was printed twice in a row: its
  queue line, held for the answer's text block (Fix 15 of the visual review), then right under it the
  delivery, so five steers took ten rows, the queue lines never on screen before. Fix 15's documented shape,
  reversed for the maintainer's rule that a sent steer shows where it was inserted.
- Red (`test '^console-(typing|render|interrupt)$'`, the new and changed tests on HEAD's code): `[ FAIL 3 |
  WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 1104 ]`: the streaming case, now three steers typed in three looks, found three
  queue lines before the three deliveries (2); the held `/status` follow-up was followed by the steer's queue
  line, not its delivery (1). The tool case (the held line before the `r` block, the delivery after the
  result) passed already.
- Green: `console_queue_line()` holds `list(rows, drop)`, `drop` for a steer not on a CLI route;
  `console_gap()` writes the held entries' rows; `console_on_queue_update()`, delivering a steer, drops every
  held entry marked `drop` before its gap, as the loop takes every queued steer at once (a drop per take would
  print the others at the first take's flush). Follow-up lines and a CLI route's steer are written as before,
  and a held steer's line still shows when a tool block starts before the boundary; below verbosity 2 no
  delivery is drawn and the guard above keeps the line. `[ FAIL 0 | WARN 0 | SKIP 1 | PASS 1107 ]`.
- Also: 04 §15.10 IC-82 (the whole-rows item), D-245's steering review note (amending the visual review's

- Plan amendment (same commit): the steering table's `Shown while queued` (D and L) and Task 10's other-output
  bullet.
- Live and visual check (the capture toolkit in the session scratchpad, `vis/`, not kept). The working tree went
  to `vis/src-s9` (`git stash create` 7b41654; its `R/` equals this commit's), installed into `vis/lib-s9`;

  - TERM=dumb, headless (tag `s9`) and in new Terminal.app windows with TERM=dumb exported in them (tags
